```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/ccs-viewer-by-cc/src/main.rs",
  "old_string": "fn main() {\n    let cli = Cli::parse();\n    let files = resolve_files(&cli);\n\n    if files.is_empty() {\n        eprintln!(\"No files to process\");\n        std::process::exit(1);\n    }\n\n    let mut total_records: usize = 0;",
  "new_string": "/// Display a session transcript: user/assistant conversation only.\nfn show_transcript(path: &PathBuf) {\n    let file = File::open(path).unwrap_or_else(|e| {\n        eprintln!(\"Error opening {}: {e}\", path.display());\n        std::process::exit(1);\n    });\n    let reader = BufReader::new(file);\n\n    for line in reader.lines() {\n        let line = line.unwrap_or_default();\n        if line.trim().is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let record: Record = match serde_json::from_str(&line) {\n            Ok(r) => r,\n            Err(_) => continue,\n        };\n        match record {\n            Record::User(user_rec) => {\n                println!(\"--- user ---\");\n                match user_rec.message.content {\n                    UserContent::Text(text) => println!(\"{text}\"),\n                    UserContent::Blocks(blocks) => {\n                        for block in blocks {\n                            match block {\n                                UserContentBlock::Text { text } => println!(\"{text}\"),\n                                UserContentBlock::ToolResult { .. } => {\n                                    println!(\"[tool_result]\");\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                println!();\n            }\n            Record::Assistant(asst_rec) => {\n                println!(\"--- assistant ---\");\n                for block in &asst_rec.message.content {\n                    match block {\n                        AssistantContentBlock::Text { text } => println!(\"{text}\"),\n                        AssistantContentBlock::ToolUse { name, .. } => {\n                            println!(\"[tool: {name}]\");\n                        }\n                        AssistantContentBlock::Thinking { .. } => {}\n                    }\n                }\n                println!();\n            }\n            _ => {}\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfn main() {\n    let cli = Cli::parse();\n    let files = resolve_files(&cli);\n\n    if files.is_empty() {\n        eprintln!(\"No files to process\");\n        std::process::exit(1);\n    }\n\n    if cli.show {\n        if files.len() > 1 {\n            eprintln!(\"--show requires a single file, got {}\", files.len());\n            std::process::exit(1);\n        }\n        show_transcript(&files[0]);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    let mut total_records: usize = 0;"
}

    25→### Options
    26→
    27→| Flag | Short | Description |
    28→|------|-------|-------------|
    29→| `--recursive` | `-r` | Treat positional args as directories, search recursively |
    30→| `--glob <PAT>` | | File pattern for recursive mode (repeatable, default: `*.jsonl`, `agent-*.meta.json`) |
    31→| `--strict` | | Exit 2 if deserialization errors are present |
    32→| `--version` | `-V` | Print version |
    33→| `--help` | `-h` | Print help |
    34→
    35→**Summary detail flags** (output order is fixed regardless of flag order):
    36→
    37→| Flag | Short | Description |
    38→|------|-------|-------------|
    39→| `--valid` | `-v` | Show valid files (path + record type counts on two lines) |
    40→| `--errors` | `-e` | Show error file paths with line numbers (or nest under groups with `-E`) |
    41→| `--error-summary` | `-E` | Show deduplicated error summary (grouped by message, sorted by count) |
    42→| `-e -E` | | Combined: grouped summary with all file paths nested under each group |
    43→| `--skipped` | `-s` | Show files that failed the first-line sniff test |
    44→| `--zero` | `-z` | Show zero-length files |

line.unwrap_or_default();\n        if line.trim().is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let record: Record = match serde_json::from_str(&line) {\n            Ok(r) => r,\n            Err(_) => continue,\n        };\n        match record {\n            Record::User(user_rec) => {\n                println!(\"--- user ---\");\n                match user_rec.message.content {\n                    UserContent::Text(text) => println!(\"{text}\"),\n                    UserContent::Blocks(blocks) => {\n                        for block in blocks {\n                            match block {\n                                UserContentBlock::Text { text } => println!(\"{text}\"),\n                                UserContentBlock::ToolResult { .. } => {\n                                    println!(\"[tool_result]\");\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                println!();\n            }\n            Record::Assistant(asst_rec) => {\n                println!(\"--- assistant ---\");\n                for block in &asst_rec.message.content {\n                    match block {\n                        AssistantContentBlock::Text { text } => println!(\"{text}\"),\n                        AssistantContentBlock::ToolUse { name, .. } => {\n                            println!(\"[tool: {name}]\");\n                        }\n                        AssistantContentBlock::Thinking { .. } => {}\n                    }\n                }\n                println!();\n            }\n            _ => {}\n        }\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Display a session transcript: user/assistant conversation only.\nfn show_transcript(path: &PathBuf) {\n    let file = File::open(path).unwrap_or_else(|e| {\n        eprintln!(\"Error opening {}: {e}\", path.display());\n        std::process::exit(1);\n    });\n    let reader = BufReader::new(file);\n    let mut shown: usize = 0;\n    let mut skipped: usize = 0;\n\n    for line in reader.lines() {\n        let line = line.unwrap_or_default();\n        if line.trim().is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let record: Record = match serde_json::from_str(&line) {\n            Ok(r) => r,\n            Err(_) => continue,\n        };\n        match record {\n            Record::User(user_rec) => {\n                if shown > 0 {\n                    println!(\"---------\");\n                }\n                println!(\"--- user ---\");\n                match user_rec.message.content {\n                    UserContent::Text(text) => println!(\"{text}\"),\n                    UserContent::Blocks(blocks) => {\n                        for block in blocks {\n                            match block {\n                                UserContentBlock::Text { text } => println!(\"{text}\"),\n                                UserContentBlock::ToolResult { .. } => {\n                                    println!(\"[tool_result]\");\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                shown += 1;\n            }\n            Record::Assistant(asst_rec) => {\n                // Collect visible content; skip records with only thinking blocks.\n                let mut lines = Vec::new();\n                for block in &asst_rec.message.content {\n                    match block {\n                        AssistantContentBlock::Text { text } => lines.push(text.clone()),\n                        AssistantContentBlock::ToolUse { name, .. } => {\n                            lines.push(format!(\"[tool: {name}]\"));\n                        }\n                        AssistantContentBlock::Thinking { .. } => {}\n                    }\n                }\n                if lines.is_empty() {\n                    skipped += 1;\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if shown > 0 {\n                    println!(\"---------\");\n                }\n                println!(\"--- assistant ---\");\n                for line in &lines {\n                    println!(\"{line}\");\n                }\n                shown += 1;\n            }\n            _ => {\n                skipped += 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    println!();\n    println!(\"Show summary: {shown} shown, {skipped} skipped\");\n}"
}

Empty assistant is gone, separators and summary working.

On why the first assistant was empty: record [2] in that session is an assistant record whose `content` array contains only a `thinking` block with an empty string (`"thinking": ""`). It's a streaming artifact — the model started a response, Claude Code logged the initial thinking phase (empty at that point), and the actual text/tool content came in subsequent records. Since we skip thinking blocks, there was nothing to print under the `--- assistant ---` header.

  "new_string": "            Record::Assistant(asst_rec) => {\n                // Collect visible content; skip records with only thinking blocks.\n                let mut lines = Vec::new();\n                let mut has_thinking = false;\n                let mut has_empty_thinking = false;\n                for block in &asst_rec.message.content {\n                    match block {\n                        AssistantContentBlock::Text { text } => lines.push(text.clone()),\n                        AssistantContentBlock::ToolUse { name, .. } => {\n                            lines.push(format!(\"[tool: {name}]\"));\n                        }\n                        AssistantContentBlock::Thinking { thinking, .. } => {\n                            has_thinking = true;\n                            if thinking.is_empty() {\n                                has_empty_thinking = true;\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                if has_thinking && has_empty_thinking {\n                    thinking_empty += 1;\n                }\n                if lines.is_empty() {\n                    thinking_only += 1;\n                    continue;\n                }"
}

{

  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/ccs-viewer-by-cc/src/main.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Display a session transcript: user/assistant conversation only.\nfn show_transcript(path: &PathBuf) {\n    let file = File::open(path).unwrap_or_else(|e| {\n        eprintln!(\"Error opening {}: {e}\", path.display());\n        std::process::exit(1);\n    });\n    let reader = BufReader::new(file);\n    let mut shown: usize = 0;\n    let mut skipped: usize = 0;\n    let mut thinking_only: usize = 0;\n    let mut thinking_empty: usize = 0;\n    let mut parse_errors: usize = 0;\n\n    for line in reader.lines() {\n        let line = line.unwrap_or_default();\n        if line.trim().is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let record: Record = match serde_json::from_str(&line) {\n            Ok(r) => r,\n            Err(_) => {\n                parse_errors += 1;\n                continue;\n            }\n        };\n        match record {\n            Record::User(user_rec) => {\n                if shown > 0 {\n                    println!(\"---------\");\n                }\n                println!(\"--- user ---\");\n                match user_rec.message.content {\n                    UserContent::Text(text) => println!(\"{text}\"),\n                    UserContent::Blocks(blocks) => {\n                        for block in blocks {\n                            match block {\n                                UserContentBlock::Text { text } => println!(\"{text}\"),\n                                UserContentBlock::ToolResult { .. } => {\n                                    println!(\"[tool_result]\");\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                shown += 1;\n            }\n            Record::Assistant(asst_rec) => {\n                // Collect visible content; skip records with only thinking blocks.\n                let mut lines = Vec::new();\n                let mut has_thinking = false;\n                let mut has_empty_thinking = false;\n                for block in &asst_rec.message.content {\n                    match block {\n                        AssistantContentBlock::Text { text } => lines.push(text.clone()),\n                        AssistantContentBlock::ToolUse { name, .. } => {\n                            lines.push(format!(\"[tool: {name}]\"));\n                        }\n                        AssistantContentBlock::Thinking { thinking, .. } => {\n                            has_thinking = true;\n                            if thinking.is_empty() {\n                                has_empty_thinking = true;\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                if has_thinking && has_empty_thinking {\n                    thinking_empty += 1;\n                }\n                if lines.is_empty() {\n                    thinking_only += 1;\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if shown > 0 {\n                    println!(\"---------\");\n                }\n                println!(\"--- assistant ---\");\n                for line in &lines {\n                    println!(\"{line}\");\n                }\n                shown += 1;\n            }\n            _ => {\n                skipped += 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    println!();\n    println!(\n        \"Show summary: {shown} shown, {thinking_only} thinking-only ({thinking_empty} empty), {skipped} skipped, {parse_errors} errors\"\n    );\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Stats returned by `show_transcript`.\n#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]\nstruct ShowStats {\n    shown: usize,\n    thinking_only: usize,\n    thinking_empty: usize,\n    skipped: usize,\n    parse_errors: usize,\n}\n\n/// Render a session transcript to `out`, returning stats.\nfn show_transcript_to(reader: impl BufRead, out: &mut impl std::io::Write) -> ShowStats {\n    let mut stats = ShowStats {\n        shown: 0,\n        thinking_only: 0,\n        thinking_empty: 0,\n        skipped: 0,\n        parse_errors: 0,\n    };\n\n    for line in reader.lines() {\n        let line = line.unwrap_or_default();\n        if line.trim().is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let record: Record = match serde_json::from_str(&line) {\n            Ok(r) => r,\n            Err(_) => {\n                stats.parse_errors += 1;\n                continue;\n            }\n        };\n        match record {\n            Record::User(user_rec) => {\n                if stats.shown > 0 {\n                    writeln!(out, \"---------\").unwrap();\n                }\n                writeln!(out, \"--- user ---\").unwrap();\n                match user_rec.message.content {\n                    UserContent::Text(text) => writeln!(out, \"{text}\").unwrap(),\n                    UserContent::Blocks(blocks) => {\n                        for block in blocks {\n                            match block {\n                                UserContentBlock::Text { text } => {\n                                    writeln!(out, \"{text}\").unwrap();\n                                }\n                                UserContentBlock::ToolResult { .. } => {\n                                    writeln!(out, \"[tool_result]\").unwrap();\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                stats.shown += 1;\n            }\n            Record::Assistant(asst_rec) => {\n                // Collect visible content; skip records with only thinking blocks.\n                let mut lines = Vec::new();\n                let mut has_thinking = false;\n                let mut has_empty_thinking = false;\n                for block in &asst_rec.message.content {\n                    match block {\n                        AssistantContentBlock::Text { text } => lines.push(text.clone()),\n                        AssistantContentBlock::ToolUse { name, .. } => {\n                            lines.push(format!(\"[tool: {name}]\"));\n                        }\n                        AssistantContentBlock::Thinking { thinking, .. } => {\n                            has_thinking = true;\n                            if thinking.is_empty() {\n                                has_empty_thinking = true;\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                if has_thinking && has_empty_thinking {\n                    stats.thinking_empty += 1;\n                }\n                if lines.is_empty() {\n                    stats.thinking_only += 1;\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if stats.shown > 0 {\n                    writeln!(out, \"---------\").unwrap();\n                }\n                writeln!(out, \"--- assistant ---\").unwrap();\n                for line in &lines {\n                    writeln!(out, \"{line}\").unwrap();\n                }\n                stats.shown += 1;\n            }\n            _ => {\n                stats.skipped += 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    stats\n}\n\n/// Display a session transcript to stdout.\nfn show_transcript(path: &PathBuf) {\n    let file = File::open(path).unwrap_or_else(|e| {\n        eprintln!(\"Error opening {}: {e}\", path.display());\n        std::process::exit(1);\n    });\n    let reader = BufReader::new(file);\n    let mut out = std::io::stdout().lock();\n    let stats = show_transcript_to(reader, &mut out);\n\n    writeln!(out).unwrap();\n    writeln!(\n        out,\n        \"Show summary: {} shown, {} thinking-only ({} empty), {} skipped, {} errors\",\n        stats.shown, stats.thinking_only, stats.thinking_empty, stats.skipped, stats.parse_errors\n    )\n    .unwrap();\n}"
}

  "new_string": "    if cli.strict && total_errors > 0 {\n        std::process::exit(2);\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use std::io::Cursor;\n\n    fn show(path: &str) -> (String, ShowStats) {\n        let file = File::open(path).expect(\"test file should exist\");\n        let reader = BufReader::new(file);\n        let mut buf = Vec::new();\n        let stats = show_transcript_to(reader, &mut buf);\n        (String::from_utf8(buf).unwrap(), stats)\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn show_basic_conversation() {\n        let (out, stats) = show(\"data/31ba272b-f7c2-436b-a017-269e27a64d07.jsonl\");\n        assert_eq!(stats.shown, 15);\n        assert_eq!(stats.thinking_only, 1);\n        assert_eq!(stats.thinking_empty, 1);\n        assert_eq!(stats.skipped, 6);\n        assert_eq!(stats.parse_errors, 0);\n        assert!(out.contains(\"--- user ---\\nrequaquaint\"));\n        assert!(out.contains(\"[tool: Bash]\"));\n        assert!(out.contains(\"[tool_result]\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn show_nonempty_thinking() {\n        let (_, stats) = show(\"data/ccs-viewer-tests.jsonl\");\n        assert_eq!(stats.thinking_only, 1);\n        assert_eq!(stats.thinking_empty, 0);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn show_mixed_errors() {\n        let (out, stats) = show(\"err-data/show-mixed.jsonl\");\n        assert_eq!(stats.shown, 3);\n        assert_eq!(stats.parse_errors, 2);\n        assert_eq!(stats.skipped, 0);\n        assert!(out.contains(\"--- user ---\\nhello\\n\"));\n        assert!(out.contains(\"--- assistant ---\\nHi there!\\n\"));\n        assert!(out.contains(\"--- user ---\\ngoodbye\\n\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn show_all_errors() {\n        let (out, stats) = show(\"err-data/bad-json.jsonl\");\n        assert_eq!(stats.shown, 0);\n        assert_eq!(stats.parse_errors, 1);\n        assert!(out.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn show_empty_input() {\n        let reader = Cursor::new(b\"\");\n        let mut buf = Vec::new();\n        let stats = show_transcript_to(reader, &mut buf);\n        assert_eq!(stats.shown, 0);\n        assert_eq!(stats.parse_errors, 0);\n        assert!(buf.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn show_separator_between_records() {\n        let (out, stats) = show(\"err-data/show-mixed.jsonl\");\n        assert_eq!(stats.shown, 3);\n        // Separator appears between records but not before the first\n        assert!(out.starts_with(\"--- user ---\\n\"));\n        assert_eq!(out.matches(\"---------\").count(), 2);\n    }\n}"
}
